Patriation And Its Consequences: Constitution Making
In Canada
Few moments in Canadian history are as intriguing as the political battle between Prime
Minister Pierre Trudeau and the “Gang of Eight” provincial premiers who opposed his plans
to “patriate” Canada's constitution from Britain. This volume revisit...
